# Design register

**Owner:** Design Director (`main-2`) · **Opened:** 2026-07-30
**States:** Exploration → Candidate → Approved (Allen) → Implemented → Design-verified

One line per item. Built from the three slices' own packages, not from the charter's summary.
Paths are worktree-relative: `[ST]` = `surething-ui`, `[RM]` = `room-refinement`, `[TV]` = `tv-sweat`,
`[M2]` = `main-2`.

**Nothing in the studio is Design-verified yet.** No implementation has been through design review
under this seat. That is the gap this register exists to close.

---

| S1 | Direction — "The Annotated Form Guide" (world 4 of 7 candidates) | Approved · Allen 2026-07-28 | `[ST] docs/design/direction-concepts/DIRECTIONS.md` |
| S2 | Artboard + legibility laws — 1024×704, 13px product-fact floor, 4.5:1, 44×32 targets, no pure black, status never colour alone | Approved · Allen 2026-07-28 | `[ST] .../SHARED-SPEC.md` **S2-am + S2-am2 · batch 20:** the 13px floor stands but was expressed in canvas space against an output-space cost — no gate ever measured it where it lands. Right-sized after the close: NOT a per-item gate; ONE recorded baseline (ramp÷stroke at the acceptance view, on the PLAYER's path, instrument first characterized against a known kernel) so a future regression has something to regress from — the studio came one walkthrough from an illegible surface no gate could see. Magnification prescription retired (the bar is met); no type re-authored, not 'not yet' but not at all on this ruling **S2-am3-cl · batch 26: the legibility claim was an ARTIFACT of the contaminated frame — STRUCK; clause 2 stands, now CALIBRATED on harness frames; the baseline instrument amends to ramp over AUTHORED stroke** **Batch 29 (item 2): the fa93238 baseline STANDS** — floor unmoved across the flood removal (authored 1.115→1.152 / 1.243→1.246; measured flat). Ruled: the baseline shoot PINS ITS SLATE; per-frame boxes are the fallback, not the method — the recipe's own C34 pin-the-seed clause applied on its first live case (a record's x tracks the team name beside it). Bounded: deal-to-deal variation is an uncharacterised term inside these numbers — under C37 no movement of ~0.037 size may be called a change or a null on this baseline until a pinned-seed assertion exists. Recipe amendment routed to room **S2-am2-am3 · batch 30: the ~0.037 bound does NOT discharge on room's record and was never room's to discharge** — the pin ELIMINATES deal-to-deal variation rather than characterising it (stronger than asked, where it applies), but (1) the S2 boxes were never measured across the pair and ramp÷stroke is non-monotonic in blur, so no pixel delta carries a derivable bound into the ratio (C41 applied on the batch that wrote it); (2) decisive: fa93238 was shot BEFORE the pin existed — no property of today's rig reaches backwards. Bound passes to SureThing's pinned re-shoot with BOTH branches pre-committed before frames land: reproduces authored values under the asserted pin → fa93238 STANDS (now pinned), bound discharges; does not → fa93238 RETIRED, the re-shoot BECOMES the baseline, no reconciliation of old numbers attempted **S2-am2-am4 · batch 31 — BRANCH 2 CONFIRMED; the ~0.037 bound is REPLACED, not tightened.** Unit check first: the report printed BOTH forms labelled per group (C33-am3) — authored form recomputed: season records 1.127 vs 1.152 (moved 0.0247), row numbers 1.246 vs 1.246 (moved 0.0002). The lane's own Part A settles it: the ratio is only meaningful against an identical string at identical size — the season-record group does NOT satisfy that across deals, so 0.0247 is the instrument read OUTSIDE ITS OWN VALIDITY, not a noise term to bound. Standing structure: across-time regression baseline = ROW NUMBERS, authored form (deal-invariant, exact); within-frame legibility (clause 2) = both groups, measured form, one frame only. Two jobs, two groups, two forms — one number spanning all of it is why ~0.037 could not be right for any of it. The 0.025 stays an OBSERVATION at n=2 deals, never promoted. fa93238 RETIRED confirmed — for batch 30's reason (shot unpinned), not the movement's size. **DD seat error (§1.5): branch 1 was DEFECTIVE** — 'reproduces → stands, now pinned' would have retroactively pinned an unpinned frame; the branches disagreed about WHY, making them two guesses filed together; corrected form: reproduces → the pinned shoot becomes baseline anyway. Cost nothing by luck, not design. Phone baseline (0.567, PHONEREF01/msgs-03) noted deal-dependent with NO invariant subgroup — never compared across time at another seed. Part A endorsed as the C37 characterization this instrument never had |

| S73 | Presenting a same-game ticket's price honestly, against a board of multipliable odds | **RULED** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 45, on the SGP lane's step-1 recommendation (`docs/sgp/`, `bbd67e6`). **The lane re-put the question correctly** — correct pricing LENGTHENS 78.07% of two-leg tickets, so the problem is not shortening but **INCONSISTENCY**: the shapes players most want to build are the ones that shorten (~32% at three legs, ρ median 1.63) while a corners-and-cards ticket lengthens, so no stable rule forms and the only available intuition (*related legs pay less*) is wrong for most tickets. **The diagnosis: TWO EFFECTS ARE SUPERIMPOSED and the surface shows only their sum** — (1) **correlation**, where related legs add less risk than they appear to and the ticket shortens, and (2) **vig**, where the naive product charges margin once per leg while the joint charges it once, lengthening everything. **The player cannot learn the rule because he sees one number containing both, and only one of the two is something he can act on.** **RULED, seven clauses.** **(1) A same-game ticket is its OWN INSTRUMENT, never a parlay with an adjustment — the surface NEVER displays a product-of-legs figure for it, and never an adjustment, a correlation discount, a was/now, or any deduction line.** This does the most work, so the reasoning is on the record: **the nerf reading is manufactured by the comparison and the comparison is manufactured by showing the product** — a surface printing *1.85 × 2.10 × 3.40 = 13.21, adjustment −32%, price 8.98* has **literally rendered a number being taken away from the player**, and no copy fixes that. There is nothing to deduct from if nothing is presented to deduct from; a same-game ticket is one bet on one compound outcome and it has A PRICE. **(2) The relationship is MARKED, in the house's own ink, which already means this:** oxide is *the house acting on the document* (S3, S15's strike its worked case) and biro is *what the player chose* — **the player picks his legs in biro, the house marks the connection between them in oxide.** Nothing is invented; this is the existing ink law doing the job it was written for, and it is the identity of the surface — **a correlation IS an annotation**, and the machine noticing that two legs tell one story is the most in-character thing this product could do with the fact. It also solves learnability with no formula: the rule becomes **"legs the house has marked pay less"**, which is true, visible and actionable. **(3) The LENGTHENING is not remarked** — no badge, no *better value*, no flag; a product congratulating itself for charging less is exhortation in the register T27 and S45 both closed. Silence is not concealment, because per clause 1 nothing ever claimed the price was a product. **(4) THE DIAL CONSTRAINT ON STEP 2, which binds pricing rather than presentation: any ticket priced materially shorter than the naive product must carry a relationship the surface can NAME.** Shortening a ticket whose relatedness cannot be marked charges the player for something he cannot see at the point of spending — **S17's class exactly**. **So the correlation model must expose a NAMEABLE RELATION, not only a scalar ρ**; the dial stays free in magnitude and is **constrained in kind**. Two costs to carry now rather than discover at step 5: the model emits a **relation label** beside its joint probability (enough to compose a sentence, not enough to be a formula), and **where it finds correlation it cannot label, the price does not move** — a deliberate constraint on pricing. **(5) Impossible combinations are refused at the slip, in words, once** — **not a disabled control** (S24 bans the disabled state outright; S56 bans a distinction in a channel the player cannot see); the leg stays reachable alone so **C19 is untouched** (the engine prices the leg, it refuses to price the COMBINATION); the refusal states the fact in the house's ink — *these two cannot both land*. **A bet that cannot win must never be purchasable:** a price is a factual claim about an outcome, and selling a finite price on an impossible event is the product lying in the one place it promised not to. **(6) An implication leg SAYS SO** — for the 22 implication shapes the second leg moves the price by roughly the margin alone, and **a leg that lands and changes nothing reads as broken and teaches a false rule**; the surface states it in the machine's voice (*this adds nothing; the first leg already contains it*), which is the same annotation channel as clause 2 and the most characterful moment in the feature. **(7) No formulae on the face** — T21's principle: values appear when the sim emits them as first-class, never computed in presentation; no ρ, no multiplier, no percentage is ever printed. **COUPLING TO ALLEN'S CALL #1, named because the lane's four questions are not independent: clause 2 is what destroys the exploit as a discoverable mechanic**, since preserving it would require the surface to HIDE the connection and clause 4's problem then returns with no remedy. **The design side of that decision, which the lane did not make: the exploit exists only under INCORRECT pricing, and incorrect pricing here means selling tickets that CANNOT WIN at finite odds up to a mean decimal of 2070.70.** That is not a discoverable mechanic — a price is a fact, and **S45 is the standing law that satire never occupies a slot where a fact belongs**. Recommended to Allen: price honestly, move the exploit to relics, **where a relic breaking a book the player has learned to trust is worth far more than a bug found in a book that was never correct**. **FLAGGED NOT RULED — the instrument needs a name:** if it is its own instrument it needs its own word and **`SGP` is industry jargon** against S22's vocabulary law; `SAME MATCH` is the obvious direction and is a fact rather than a brand — **material naming is Allen's** (S16, S46 both his), raised now so step 2 does not bake `SGP` into player-facing strings. **TV CONSEQUENCE noted, not ruled:** a same-game ticket's legs appear in the TV ticket column during the sweat and **the mark must survive that trip or the two surfaces disagree about what the ticket is**; TV's leg rows are ruled at T24/T40/G1 with its own ink register, so how the mark renders there is TV's question — scheduled rather than discovered, and it does not block step 2 **S73-am · batch 46 — Q1/Q2/Q3 ruled on the lane's sharper brief, DRAWS-AWARE; batch 45's DIAGNOSIS is superseded and every ruling in it survives on stronger ground.** **The diagnosis first:** batch 45 built the learnability problem on *two superimposed effects* — correlation shortening, **vig lengthening everything** — which was correct for the state in `step-1-recommendation.md` (78.07% lengthen at `Ω_sgp = Ω`). **The ADOPTED margin rule removes the vig half:** `shown price ÷ product = 1/ρ`, so **51.4% of two-leg tickets are exactly independent and price IDENTICALLY to the multiplied number.** Recorded as a **moving input, not a misreading** — the dial was set after the document ruled on — and the consequence is that **the divergence is no longer a mixture but a clean signal appearing ONLY where correlation exists**, which is the condition clause 4 was written to require. **One thing gets harder and is named: intermittent correctness teaches a habit more strongly than uniform divergence** — the player multiplies, is **exactly right 51.4% of the time**, then is silently wrong by ~39% on the shapes he most wants to build; a rule that works half the time is learned as a rule that works. **Q1 — RULED: the qualitative marker, option 3, no arithmetic.** Not *show nothing* (the brief makes the case itself — a player who multiplies and finds a discrepancy has found a REAL one and deserves an answer; silence is **S17's class**). Not *show both numbers* — it makes the slip a calculator (**T21**), renders the same fact twice (**S58**), and puts a number visibly moving on the document; it would not always be a deduction, since negatively correlated tickets pay MORE, but a two-directional correction line is **worse**, teaching the player to watch a number change rather than read his ticket. **What the mark buys is the whole argument: the player's complete and CORRECT mental model needs no arithmetic — *unmarked legs multiply; marked legs pay less*.** The mark is what **makes** the multiplication rule work rather than what replaces it, telling him exactly when his own arithmetic applies. **The fairness fact is NOT printed:** the brief offers that a same-game ticket carries the same house edge as an equivalent cross-game parlay and that this is defensible — it is true, and it stays off the slip, because **a house that explains its own fairness at the point of spending reads as a house with something to explain** (S44's boundary — the machine does not wear the house's brand, and the house does not testify about itself on the player's document). **Q2 — RULED: WARN. Not block, not silence.** **Silence refused outright** — the brief's own *"quietly milked by a trap they cannot see"* is **S17 verbatim**, and it is the most corrosive thing available to a product whose promise is that the numbers do not lie. **Blocking refused, and NOT on paternalism, which is the weaker argument: a redundant leg is a LEGAL bet, correctly priced.** This game is about a player learning to read a book — **a house that prevents you from being stupid is not this product; a house that tells you and lets you do it anyway is exactly this product** — and blocking forecloses uses nobody has anticipated (a relic, a challenge condition). **The clutter objection dissolves:** the warning appears on **22 shapes**, not on every slip. Copy states the fact naming both legs — *`OVER 1.5 GOALS adds nothing — OVER 2.5 GOALS already contains it`* — **and it is the most credible thing in the feature because it costs the house money to say**, which is what makes the rest of the machine believable. **Q3 — RULED DRAWS-AWARE; the trap does not partially dissolve, it GOES.** Allen greenlit draws today, and **both artefact shapes were impossible only because 1–1 was unrepresentable** — with draws, `BTTS YES + Under 2.5` is a legal, ordinary, popular ticket; not refused, no copy written, nothing left to expose. **STANDING RULE, bigger than the two shapes: the surface refuses only what is impossible in FOOTBALL, never what is merely impossible in the MODEL; where the model is more restrictive than the game it simulates, the MODEL is the defect and the refusal does not ship.** **Why it is a law and not a copy preference: a refusal is the machine making a factual claim about the world**, and if it says *these cannot both happen* about something real football does every weekend, a football-literate player catches the product lying — **and every other number the machine shows loses its credit at the same moment. This surface's authority is that it does not lie about facts, and that authority is indivisible.** It generalises past draws, since any v1 simplification manufactures the same false refusals: **before a refusal ships it is checked against football, not against the model**, and the check states what it could not cover (C18 §4.2). **STEP-3 CONSEQUENCE, the build reach the brief predicted: the impossible set is RE-DERIVED after draws land** — the 22 two-leg and 57 three-leg shapes were enumerated against a drawless model and that enumeration is now stale **in the direction that matters, over-refusing**; every shape re-checked, the two known artefacts expected to leave. **The refusal reads:** *`OVER 2.5 GOALS and UNDER 1.5 GOALS cannot both land`* — the fact, both legs named, once, at the slip in its own space; **not a modal** (S55), **not a disabled control** (S24, S56), no scolding (S71/S72 — name the state, not the owner). It teaches because it is **specific**: the player learns which two legs conflict, which is real football knowledge he keeps. **THE INK SPLIT, stated because three annotations now exist and will otherwise be muddled: OXIDE for the correlation link** (the house's mark ON the player's picks — S3's own definition, the dead-leg strike's family); **TONER for both statements** — *adds nothing* and *cannot both land* — (the machine stating a fact in its own space, S26's *state rather than exhort*); **BIRO for none of them, ever**, biro being only what the player chose, with S26 the worked case of getting exactly this wrong. The distinction that keeps oxide from diluting: **oxide marks the house acting on his picks; toner is the machine saying something true — a refusal is not the house acting, it is arithmetic, spoken** **S73-am2 · batch 47 — ALLEN'S DECISION: THE HONEST BOOK IS YES.** The coupling named at S73 resolves in the direction recommended — **the mark ships, the exploit dies.** Clause 2 is unblocked and step 2 proceeds under clause 4's constraint: the model emits a **relation label** beside its joint, and **where it cannot label a correlation the price does not move.** **Consequence ROUTED, not executed:** `02-betting-math.md:24` carries the *"correlated parlay as discoverable exploit"* framing and that framing is now dead — it is a **shared canonical document, edited at integration and not by this seat** (STUDIO.md's ownership rule), so it is named as an owed edit rather than made; the replacement is on the record at S73, **the exploit moves to relics, where a relic breaking a book the player has learned to trust is worth more than a bug found in a book that was never correct.** **STILL ALLEN'S AND STILL OPEN: the mark's name**, held for him as is the instrument's (`SGP` is jargon against S22; `SAME MATCH` offered as the direction) — **named here so step 2 bakes neither into player-facing strings**, and the build can proceed on placeholders since neither name changes a mechanism **S73-am3 · batch 48 — ALLEN'S TWO NAMES, INTO CANON: the instrument is `SAME MATCH`; the mark is `THE HOUSE'S LINE`. Player-facing strings may be authored REAL from this line — no placeholders on either.** **Why the mark's name is better than the brief that asked for it, recorded because the lane builds against it: `THE HOUSE'S LINE` is true in BOTH registers at once** — it is *literally* a line the house draws on the document connecting two of the player's picks, and *"the line"* is already the betting word for what the book says. **The name is simultaneously the physical mark and the house's position, which is exactly what the mark is** — the annotated-form-guide identity in three words, arriving with nothing to explain. It sits correctly against **S3** (oxide is *the house's mark*): the mark now has a name that says whose it is, in the surface's vocabulary rather than the industry's. **RULED, because recording a name without ruling where it appears leaves the lane to guess and the wrong guess is specific and likely — `THE HOUSE'S LINE` printed beside the mark on every slip. THE MARK IS DRAWN, NOT CAPTIONED.** That would be **the house captioning its own annotation** — the move already refused at Q1 for the fairness fact, and **S44's boundary again: the machine does not narrate the house's presence on the player's document**; a mark needing a label on every occurrence is also a mark that is not working. **(1) The oxide line is drawn and carries NO LABEL** — it connects the picks and says what it says. **(2) Where a statement is needed it states the RELATION, not the name** — S73 clause 4 already requires the model to emit a relation label *"enough to compose a sentence"*, and this is that sentence: **what the legs SHARE**, in toner, in the machine's voice, once per slip, never announcing the apparatus. **(3) `THE HOUSE'S LINE` is what the thing is CALLED** — used where the product refers to it (rules copy, the ledger, a first-encounter explanation) and **never as a per-occurrence caption.** **The onboarding gap is real and is answered by the STATEMENT, not the name:** the first time a player sees an oxide line he cannot decode it (S56's class), and **the toner sentence is what teaches it, on the slip where it happens, in the same breath as the mark** — the name is how he refers to it afterwards. **`SAME MATCH` form:** uppercase as the surface's market vocabulary already is (`OVER 2.5 GOALS`, `MUSKRATS ML`), **a role printed as a word** per S22's own requirement, a fact rather than a brand; tracking follows the laptop's label treatment under `surething-design.md` §4.3 with **the token the kit's to supply, not this seat's to invent** (S70's endorsed precedent — leave it untracked rather than guess). **Nothing else moves: both names are VOCABULARY, and neither changes a mechanism, a price, a refusal, or a mark's ink** — S73 and S73-am stand exactly as ruled **S73-am4 · batch 55 — THE OWNING-DOC FOLD SURFACED A CONFLICT AND THE OWNING DOC WINS (§1.2): the impossibility refusal takes STAMP, not toner, and must carry CAUSE AND REMEDY.** Batch 46's ink split put both statements in toner on the reasoning that *a refusal is not the house acting, it is arithmetic spoken*. **`surething-design.md` §3.3 already rules the state**: **Blocked = muted label + STAMPED literal reason ≥13px, CAUSE AND REMEDY, inside the control** — and **a refused leg combination IS a Blocked state**, so it takes the treatment the surface already has rather than a new one. **Two corrections, both this seat's: (1) the ink** — Stamp, not toner, because the house declining to take a bet is the house acting on the document, which is Stamp's own definition; **(2) the copy** — batch 46's `X and Y cannot both land` gives the CAUSE only, where this row has always required **cause AND remedy**, so the refusal also names what to drop. **Constitution §1.2 decides it without argument** — the owning document outranks the register's ruling for that item on that surface's content — and **§6's *a clause that contradicts an owning document is a conflict to escalate, not a licence* is why this surfaced at the fold rather than in a build.** **UNCHANGED and re-confirmed against the doc: the implication warning is NOT a Blocked state and does NOT take Stamp** — a legal-but-pointless leg is legal, correctly priced and added, and the machine states the fact **in toner, in its own space**; blocking it stays refused (*a house that prevents him from being stupid is not this product*), and silence stays refused as S17's invisible cost. **`THE HOUSE'S LINE` sits correctly as a named use of Stamp** — §3.1's third row doing the job it was written for — **drawn, never captioned.** **The value of folding, recorded: a ruling that never meets the owning document never meets its own surface's existing law.** | batch 45 |
| S74 | The draw on the board, and the forms it needs | **RULED** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 49, on the draws work (`markets-pregame` D1, `3dc7a03` — *DRAWS. The moneyline is 1X2*). **The engine fact that decides it: `MatchResult {Home, Draw, Away}` while `Side` STAYS TWO-VALUED as a TEAM designator, and `Pick.Side`/`Leg.Side` now THROW on Draw. The draw is NOT a team** — the engine ruled that structurally and **the surface must not undo it in presentation**; a draw rendered as a third team is a category error the code has already refused. **S24 predicted this exact moment** (*paired pricing describes two-outcome markets; scorer renders as a single-column offer list, never a paired row with a dead cell*): **the moneyline is no longer a two-outcome market**, so the paired row no longer describes it and must not be forced to. **RULED: three offers, the draw in the MIDDLE** — its position is not borrowed convention but **meaning**: the draw sits between the two teams because it is the outcome where **neither wins**, and putting it at either end would make it look like a third competitor, which is exactly what `Side` refuses to let it be. **Named as what it is — `DRAW` — never as a team and never with a team's treatment: no pitch dot** (T2 gives muted blue and pink to the two sides; a draw has no side and takes no hue), no crest, nothing team-coloured. **`1X2` NEVER REACHES THE PLAYER** — industry jargon exactly as `SGP` was, and **S22 governs**: the surface composes and the role is printed as a word; `1X2` is a code word for a market shape and stays in the code. **G1's DECK OWES A DRAW FORM AND THIS IS THAT AUTHORING** — G1's scope line said *a seventh market needs a form authored before it ships*, and while the draw is not a seventh market but a **third outcome on an existing one**, the moneyline's authored forms **assume a team** (`MIDDLEMEN ML`) and a draw leg has none. **The word already exists on this surface: T62 records the live leg reading `LEVEL 0–0 at 14`, so `LEVEL` is already the product's word for a tied scoreline** — nothing is invented. **NEED** (requirement, while live) = `LEVEL AT FULL TIME`; **progress** (state, beneath) = `LEVEL` / `NOT LEVEL`; **compact** (identity elsewhere) = `DRAW`. **T70's pair check passes by T70-am's own test rather than by luck** — the check is an INFORMATION test, not word-overlap, and *shared words carrying different facts are legal*: `LEVEL AT FULL TIME` is a requirement at full time, `LEVEL` is the state right now. **Fit is NOT asserted** — these are new strings in the canon face, they measure against their columns like everything else, they join the sweep's population under C46, and any overrun routes to T74 with the rest | batch 49 |

DD §1.5 correction: its four "wall" regions were the TV's own housing; conclusion unaffected (point-light falloff +44.5→+20.0→+9.5, away-facing +1.8) **Batch 34 — the owed settlement capture MAY ALREADY EXIST.** Across the Phase T before-set's 151 in-room frames the room wall is constant at ~(30.2, 51.8, 34.1) and does NOT track panel brightness — except at the two payoff beats (`t68am-accept-slot`, `t71-win-tally-slot`) where it rises to ~(51.4, 65.1, 43.2) / ~(43.8, 62.0, 40.6): `RoomSettlementGlow()` firing on settlement and nowhere else, **T65 built as ruled**, on a pinned in-room set. **Deliberately NOT measured at the DD seat** — an emissive re-tint's hue/chroma is CIELAB on linear authored values under C33-am3, and those figures are crude display-encoded RGB from a different instrument in a different space; the three ladders are never compared. Routed to room + TV to measure in the correct space and close the provisional (88.0°, 0.9) if it holds. Named so no one shoots a settlement window that already exists **T65-am · batch 36 — the palette constraint is VIOLATED; the provisional (88.0°, 0.9) is RETIRED — it was never a CIELAB hue.** Room measured the authored constant in the space C33-am3 prescribes for this exact quantity (emission hue/chroma = CIELAB on linear authored) and routed it for a ruling it declined to make; measurement ACCEPTED in full. `roomSettlementWarm = (0.818, 1.000, 0.610)` measures **125.7° CIELAB, chroma 26.62** (×0.9 → 125.7°/25.70/L\* 93.44); the room's warm family is **83–85°** — `room-design.md`'s own R41 (*"the rust end or the screens' 83–85°, never signal-red"*), with a representative warm emission at 83.3° beside lid 85.1–85.3°, phone 85.4°, laptop 84.3°. **~41° past the family, toward green**; robust to the authoring-space question (linear vs sRGB moves it 0.3°). **Room hedged that this turns on V6's gate question — IT DOES NOT, on two independent grounds.** (1) **A gate passing is not a constraint satisfied:** clause 4 is *"stays inside the room's palette"* and V6 is only the instrument meant to bound it; a gate that passes a value outside the band it was drawn for is a blind gate, not a grant (C18 §4.2, the sixth-plus vacuous green). The gate's state decides whether the GATE needs repair, never whether the VALUE is in the palette. (2) **Decisive — the value is outside the room's warm family in BOTH spaces:** HSV 88° is yellow-green *by construction of the hexcone* (60° yellow, 120° green, amber 30–45°), and the one room emission whose triple room supplied, `(0.038, 0.032, 0.024)`, returns **34.3° HSV** — so the family sits near 34° HSV and 83–85° CIELAB while the authored value sits at 88° and 125.7°. **The space confusion explains how it PASSED; it did not create the failure** — `88.0` matched the numerals of a band drawn in another space, a coincidence of digits, not of colour. **Ruled — HUE ONLY, one variable:** the re-tint moves to **CIELAB hue 83–85°** per R41-am's standing form (*the swatch supplies hue and chroma; luminance is the element's own*). **Chroma NOT re-opened** — authored 26.62 held and rotated at constant C\*; the rendered delta (chroma +2.8, L\* +3.9) was never the defect, and bounding authored chroma against the room's RENDERED emitter band (≈5) is the two-instruments error this lane has paid for twice. **The firing half STANDS, GRANTED** — fires on the two settlement beats and nowhere else, both agreeing to two decimals on a pinned in-room set; clause 4's *"fires on settlement, not per leg"* holds, and room's note that both beats are LOSSES (`grammar-BreakawayAgainst`, `grammar-LegFinalLost`) confirms it is keyed to the moment, not to a win. **Batch 13's "~85–92°" is RESTATED to the owning doc's 83–85° (§1.5, this seat's)** — written four batches before C33-am3 with NO SPACE NAMED, and its upper half brackets no measured member (C18 §4.1); the owning document postdates it and already carries the family, so the stray number conforms to the document rather than a new decision being made. **Why green is wrong on design, not only arithmetic:** `TvLight` is C2's green — the SCREEN's hue — so a settlement glow at 125.7° makes the room answer the payoff moment by taking on the television's own colour, losing its own response at the one instant it most needs one. **Still owed, unchanged:** the cast on the derivation's own `housing above panel` box shot under **R23's recipe with `TvLight` disabled** is TV's half of the joint task and is NOT satisfied here — room correctly declined the rendered absolutes (confounded by the live panel green, §2.6; the DELTAS are sound evidence of firing, the ABSOLUTES close nothing). **Coupled:** the `[0.78, 1.06]` amplitude window sits in the same source comment as the 88.0° and re-derives with the conversion — ruled with V6, not assumed here **T65-am2 · batch 37 — batch 36's TARGET was wrong; the VERDICT is unchanged (§1.5, this seat's).** Batch 36 restated the band from "~85–92°" to **83–85°**, reasoning that its upper half bracketed no measured member. **Wrong, and V6's provenance is what shows it:** the band has two traced anchors — **laptop lid 85.1–85.3° at the bottom and the warm key tube ~92° at the top**, both rendered CIELAB, both from batch-13 records. **The key IS the upper anchor**; the band was correctly drawn. **The restatement to 83–85° is WITHDRAWN and the ruled band 85–92° stands as written.** Cause: batch 36 read the family off room's T65 record, which lists the SCREENS (lid, phone, laptop) and not the key — the key entered the record one docket later, in V6's trace. **Second error, the same class this seat warned room about in the same batch:** the band is a **RENDERED** band and `roomSettlementWarm` is an **AUTHORED** constant, so telling the lane to author to 83–85° compared an authored value against a rendered bound. **Corrected — the authored constant is the KNOB; the ruled band 85–92° is checked on the RENDERED CAST**, on TV's own box under R23 with `TvLight` disabled (the capture already owed, now carrying the check); **nobody authors to the band's number and assumes it renders there** — the authored↔rendered gap is real and non-uniform (1.9° on the lid, 10.7° on the key). **Unchanged and in force:** the violation itself (125.7° sits 33.7° past the band's top and is outside the room's warm family in BOTH spaces), the retirement of the provisional (88.0°, 0.9), hue-only with chroma not re-opened, the firing half granted, and T80-am's freeze. **What the pair of errors argues:** batch 36's finding that the VERDICT is independent of V6 was sound and stands, and room's hedge was aimed at the verdict where it was over-cautious — but the coupling that mattered ran to the **REMEDY**, and neither seat named it. *A coupled docket can be independent for the verdict and dependent for the fix* **T65-am3 · batch 51 — THE SUBJECT STAYS UNADJUDICATED; THE VIOLATION SURVIVES; THE TARGET IS VOID.** Room's R23 pair fired **disposition 3** and **the subject's 87.3–92.7 is NOT a pass and must not become one by the band moving underneath it** — returned unadjudicated (§2.6), re-judged **only** against a re-derived band (V6-am) on a run where the anchors separate. **Two things must not be quietly harvested from this: (1) the subject was never measured against a working instrument** — a band whose top is void cannot pass anything, and **a subject that happens to sit inside a WRONG band has not conformed to a RIGHT one**; **(2) resolution is still unsolved** — subject spread at **77% of the band** is C32's problem wherever the top lands, so the re-run addresses **signal**, not only the bound. **WHAT SURVIVES, stated because the arc otherwise looks unravelled: T65-am's VIOLATION FINDING STANDS.** `roomSettlementWarm` measures **125.7°**, **~24° above even the corrected anchor** and outside the family under **every** candidate top — 83–85, 85–92, or a band reaching 101.4. **The violation, the retirement of the provisional (88.0°, 0.9), hue-only with chroma not re-opened, and the granted firing half are all UNTOUCHED.** **What is void is the TARGET:** the remedy's direction — move toward the room's warm family — survives, **the number to move to does not exist yet and is not invented here**, and **per C41 the expectation stays a DIRECTION OF TRAVEL and never a value**, which is the clause keeping this ruling from making the same mistake a third time **T65-am4 · batch 52 — THE SUBJECT'S HUE HAS NEVER BEEN MEASURED, and batch 51's survival claim was WRONG (§1.5, third instance in this chain, all this seat's).** **The asymmetry that gives it away:** the key tube measures **authored 102.7° / rendered 101.4°, a gap of 1.3°**, while `roomSettlementWarm` measures **authored 125.7° / rendered 87.3–92.7°, a gap of ~35°** — **same instrument, same run, same space.** A 1.3° gap on one element and ~35° on another **is not a property of the elements; it is the two numbers measuring different things.** **THE DIAGNOSIS: the housing's absolute hue is a MIXTURE.** With `TvLight` disabled the panel green is gone, **but the key tube still lights that housing** — so the rendered figure is *the housing under key PLUS glow*, not *the glow*, and **an absolute on a lit surface can never isolate one contributor.** **THIS SEAT'S OWN R23 SPEC IS DEFECTIVE ON THE POINT:** Route A (absolute on the housing, `TvLight` disabled) was written as the RULED route and Route B (the on/off delta) as a fallback for a *different* reason — the coupling precondition. **Route B was the correct PRIMARY all along**, only the delta isolating the glow's contribution, and **room's instinct was right twice over**: T65-am already recorded that *the DELTAS are sound evidence and the ABSOLUTES close nothing*, **and this seat then built a spec whose primary route produces an absolute.** **THE CORRECTION TO BATCH 51:** it stated the violation survives because *125.7° is outside every candidate band* — which **compared an AUTHORED value against a RENDERED reference, the exact error T65-am2 identified and corrected, made again in the batch that was correcting the same class of error.** **RULED — the honest state, which is neither the violation standing nor the violation dead: the authored constant is 125.7°** (a fact about the code, not in dispute); **the glow's RENDERED hue is UNMEASURED** — not by the authored value (the knob, per T65-am2) and not by the housing absolute (a mixture); **so the violation is UNPROVEN, not disproven** — not withdrawn, and **not to be harvested as a pass**; **and a NEW finding stands on its own — the authored↔rendered relationship for this element is UNEXPLAINED**, 35° where a comparable element shows 1.3°, and **until that is explained neither number can serve as the check**, the explanation being worth having whichever way the verdict falls. **OWED: the DELTA** — glow-on minus glow-off, one frame, pinned and asserted, converted through the shared `linear_to_lab`, so the glow's own chromaticity is the difference's and the mixture problem disappears. **IT MAY ALREADY EXIST: if two halves of the four-half capture on main are glow-on and glow-off under one recipe, the delta computes from frames already shot and NO WINDOW IS NEEDED** — check that first, per the standing practice of checking existing crops before booking a capture; **if it does not exist, that is the window to book, and it is the only thing on this item needing one.** **Unchanged and not re-opened: the firing half (granted), the retirement of the provisional (88.0°, 0.9), chroma not re-opened — and the risk line, THE TOP BEING WRONG DOES NOT MAKE THE SUBJECT CONFORM. It still does not. It now also does not make it fail. BOTH readings are void and that is the actual position** **T65-cl · batch 53 — THE SUBJECT RULED; T65's REMAINING HALF CLOSES ON THE PROPERTY, and the numeric floor is RETIRED as a check on this element.** Room delivered both items without a window (`65858f8`). **ROOM'S REFINEMENT ADOPTED IN FULL, and it is better than this seat's reasoning (§1.5, fourth in this chain and the FIRST the lane corrected rather than the seat):** batch 52 carved the population **lights vs screens**, and **`PhoneBuzzLight` falsifies that in one member** — it is a **light**, it sits at **83.3°** with the screens, **and it does so because it IS the screen emitter rescaled.** **The correct carve is FIXTURE FAMILY — what shares an authored source belongs together** — and room's own sentence is the sharpest thing in the docket: ***clustering cannot defend the split, its own reasoning does.*** This seat argued from clustering (2° apart, therefore a family); **clustering is a CONSEQUENCE, not a cause**, and it cannot defend the split precisely because a LIGHT clusters with the screens. R39's shared base was always the cause and R39 said so (*closes by construction, `Amp(1/3/15)` off one shared base*). **The conclusion survives; the argument for it was wrong.** **Families on the right axis:** the screen emitter and everything rescaled from it (lid, laptop, phone, **`PhoneBuzzLight`**) at **83.3–85.4°**; the warm key tube at **101.4°**; `ArtIndicator` at **49.7°** (rust law); `WindowGlow` at **77.0°** (textured, the map governs, not a palette member). **WHICH FAMILY THE SUBJECT IS IN: NONE.** `roomSettlementWarm` is **its own authored constant**, neither rescaled from the screen base nor the key — **its own fixture, belonging to no existing family** — **which dissolves the question the band was asked to answer**, since a band bounds a family and **four batches were spent measuring this element against a family it was never a member of.** **THE ARITHMETIC, TAKEN AT FACE VALUE:** the subject sits **8.7° below at nearest, 14.1° at farthest**, the tolerance interval is **96.0–106.8**, and the nearest reading is **3.3° below the floor**. **That failure is NOT RESOLVABLE and this seat will not pretend it is** — a **3.3°** miss against a subject spread of **5.4°** is **smaller than the instrument's own noise**, which is disposition 3's resolution problem re-expressed exactly as room says: **a gate cannot fail a value by less than it can measure.** **But resolution is not the ruling, because the proxy is the wrong instrument regardless. C40 GOVERNS AND THIS IS ITS CONVERSE:** a passing proxy is never read as the property held, so **a FAILING proxy must equally not be read as the property VIOLATED.** **The property clause 4 protects is that the room's response to settlement is the room's OWN warmth, not a foreign colour** — and the glow renders at **87.3–92.7°, BETWEEN the room's two warm fixtures** (screen family 83.3–85.4, key 101.4), inside the room's own warm span, and **33–38° away from the TV green that motivated the original finding**, whose design argument was *a settlement glow at 125.7° makes the room answer the payoff moment by taking on the television's own colour*. **It does not. It answers in the room's own warmth. RULED: the property holds; T65's remaining half CLOSES.** **What the close does NOT do: it certifies no number, it does not resurrect the band as a bound on this element, and it does not convert a 3.3° miss into a pass** — it rules that **the element was measured against the wrong KIND of instrument, and that the thing the instrument stood in for is satisfied on the frames.** **Retired with it: the numeric floor as a check on `roomSettlementWarm`.** **ONE THING STAYS OPEN AND IT IS NOT BLOCKING — the authored↔rendered gap, 125.7° authored to 87.3–92.7° rendered, ~35°**, most likely **the grade**, which is exactly what T65-am2 warned of when it ruled *nobody authors to the band's number and assumes it renders there*; **the close does not depend on explaining it** because the law was always checked on the rendered cast, **but it is a standing hazard: on this element an authored value lands ~35° from where it renders, so nobody may author to a rendered target here without characterising the transfer first.** Nothing pending needs it |

the real widest form is `UNDER 10.5 CORNERS` at 144.3 in a 147.0 box. **The disposition pre-committed at T89 fires exactly as written: the relief was UNNECESSARY, not mis-sized — it STANDS, the box re-verifies, and it re-verifies as FITTING.** No revert, no fresh docket, phase untouched. **The traceability pass earned its place on this one row alone** — it removed a slot from the overrun table by finding that **the string it failed on could not exist**; overrunning slots **7 → 5**, and one of the two that left did so **because a measurement was wrong rather than because anything was fixed** **T74-am4 · batch 57 — `RiskPays`: THE WORD *UNBOUNDED* IS THE WRONG ONE, and the composition is the fourth instance of one answer.** `PotentialPayout` is parlay-multiplied so the sweep correctly reports no bounded worst case — **but parlay multiplication is not UNBOUNDED, it is UN-ENUMERATED.** `MaxLegs` is finite (4), the stake has a ceiling, and every leg's odds come from a pricing model with its own range, so **the largest renderable payout is ARITHMETIC — a product of finite terms.** **Nobody has computed it, which is a different problem from it not existing**, and it is C18 §4.1's one level up: an inventory that does not name its members. **This seat is not designing around infinity when the number is a multiplication away.** **OWED, and cheap: the maximum renderable payout, computed from `MaxLegs`, the stake ceiling and the odds range** — then the box is sized against **the longest renderable form** (C46, T89-B) and this stops being a design question at all. **COMPOSITION RULED: `RISK` and `PAYS` take SEPARATE ROWS.** `RISK $1,234  PAYS $12,340` is **two labels and two figures in one row** at 296.5 in a 249.0px box — **N independent strings are not one row**, the fourth instance today after the intervention prompt, the money control and `TakeoverSub`; each figure then has the row's full width instead of sharing it with a sibling whose slack is its overrun. **ANCHORING, taken from this surface's OWN existing pattern: label left, figure RIGHT-ANCHORED.** Not invented — it is the clock's shape measured on the Phase T pair (T75-am2: *right-anchored, ink right edge constant while the left edge travels*): **a right-anchored figure grows LEFTWARD into its own row's slack rather than overflowing rightward off the box**, so the binding constraint becomes *figure meets label* rather than *figure meets box edge*, which is far later and is a constraint the composition can state. **It also pairs correctly with tabular figures** — T82 made digit advances equal, so a right-anchored tabular figure grows in **exact digit-width steps** and the label's clearance is predictable rather than content-dependent. **`RISK` is the stable half** (bounded by the stake ceiling) **and `PAYS` the variable one**, so only one row needs the headroom. **DISPOSITIONS PRE-COMMITTED on the number nobody has computed yet: the maximum renderable payout fits its right-anchored row → the box is sized against it, recorded as the longest renderable form, and this CLOSES with no further ruling; it does not fit → the span is re-derived ONCE AT DESIGN TIME** (§5.1 makes that explicitly legal) **or the composition changes again — and ABBREVIATION STAYS REFUSED IN BOTH BRANCHES**, a span that cannot be found making this a **C16 classification** where a wider row is a design cost and not a platform impossibility: **expensive, never impossible. Neither branch reopens C49** |

| T82 | The mandatory tabular figures are unreachable on the shipped stack | **RULED — the mandate STANDS unamended; the atlas ships the font's own tabular glyphs; the "cannot" is EXPENSIVE, not impossible** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 38, on TV's three-way measurement at `cb84278`. **Measurement ACCEPTED in full, and it corroborates harder than the docket claimed.** Three instruments, two sharing no machinery: an in-engine digit probe, `hmtx` advances read through `cmap` with no editor, and a GSUB enumeration — which is why the finding survives its own editor crash, named here as the discipline to copy. The docket left the file-versus-engine gap as a hand-wave (*"≈87 px against the 93 px measured, the difference being the instance"*); **checked at this seat it is exact.** Predicting each measured spread from `hmtx` and taking the ratio gives **1.0704 at Regular 36pt AND 1.0704 at Regular 28pt; 0.998 at Condensed 29pt AND 0.998 at Condensed 19pt** — **constant across sizes within each face, to four figures**, which is the signature of a uniform instance scale and therefore exactly the cause the docket named. The two instruments do not roughly agree, they agree exactly up to one scale factor per face, and **that scale factor IS the instance** (Condensed renders its file default; Regular renders an instance ~7% wider in advance). Proportionality is over-determined, not merely corroborated. **C44-clean** — reported as independent corroboration with the residual named, never as a bound judging a reading. **Classification (C16), because the headline and the body disagree:** the title says tabular *cannot be reached*; the body says *"unreachable by any in-scope choice"* and, decisively, *"closing it requires a decision about how the glyphs are obtained rather than a line of code"* — **that sentence IS C16's classification, stated by the lane, and it is adopted as this ruling's reasoning.** `tnum` at runtime is genuinely **impossible** (TMP's `OTL_FeatureTag` declares only `kern`, `liga`, `mark`, `mkmk`; no tag or property exposes one) — but tabular figures on this surface are **possible**, because the glyphs are drawn, present in GSUB and addressable at build time. Therefore **expensive, not impossible: no signed deviation is issued and none is needed.** The headline overshoots the body; recorded as a classification correction and nothing more — the lane surfaced the refuting route itself and recommended it, the opposite of a finding that hides its own escape. **The mandate is NOT amended:** the twitch is 46–93 px across ten digits at rendered sizes, not marginal; **T11 selected the face on this exact criterion** with Saira disqualified for lacking `tnum`, so amending now would retroactively decide the criterion the face was chosen on does not matter; and a faithful route exists — C16 does not let convenience reclassify itself as impossibility. **Option (b) `<mspace>` REFUSED** — forcing one advance imposes a metric **the family does not contain**, which is §4's *no synthesised styling* in the spacing channel (a synthesised bold is a smear, an italic a shear, a monospace the third of the same thing), and it hits letters in mixed strings: `CASH OUT $183` is the money control that cost four batches at T63/T68/T68-am/T71, and monospacing its label to fix its figures is **C10's shape exactly**. **Option (c) amend-the-mandate REFUSED, held as the named fallback with its condition PRE-COMMITTED before the build attempt:** if (a) proves unbuildable — the substitution cannot be resolved at generation time, or the tabular glyphs are not addressable in the generated set despite their GSUB presence — the classification changes to genuinely impossible and (c) returns as a **C16 signed deviation with a named cost and an expiry**; not before, and never on difficulty. **Option (a) GRANTED as the route** — the atlas ships the font's own tabular digits as its default set, the substitution resolved at build time with U+0030–0039 mapped to the tabular glyph indices. These are the figures **the type designer drew**, which is what separates it from (b). **The ruling is the OUTCOME, not the mechanism:** either the Unity generator resolving the substitution or a pre-processed static instance with the feature frozen and committed as the atlas source — **the lane picks**; this seat does not choose a build method. **Four conditions.** (1) **The unit is the ASSET, not the slot** — T75-am established it and this confirms it one level down; **every generated asset any figure slot renders on takes the tabular set, and the inventory NAMES ITS MEMBERS** (C18 §4.1), not "the atlas": the condensed Bold 700 asset carries `CashOut`, the surface's money figure, and an inventory naming two assets while missing a third ships a control that still twitches. (2) **Digits alone satisfy the mandate** — digits are the characters that change, `$`, `:` and separators are constant and need nothing; the mandate's content is **equal advance among digits**, not constant string width, so a score going 9 to 10 adds a character and widens and that is layout (T75-am2's right-anchor), never a tabular failure. (3) **One variable** — `lnum` is also present; figure STYLE does not move in this commit, tabular only. (4) **§3.5 binds the same commit** — a width change obliges the layout depending on it to be re-derived in the same commit, and a zone that cannot hold the widened figure routes to T74, never a tune inside the tabular commit. **Licensing: no new question** (same OFL 1.1 files already subsetted into SDF atlases; a build-time feature application is the same class of operation on the same files) — informational, since T11 recorded a licence basis. **Free by-product, ROUTED not ruled:** the constant per-face ratio is a **width-axis fingerprint of the rendered instance obtained without frames**, which is the desk measurement T78-am owes — bounded twice so nobody over-reads it: **advance alone cannot separate weight from width** (a heavier instance at one width is wider too, which is why T78-am asked for stem width AND width axis; this supplies one channel), and **which asset `cb84278` loaded decides what the number means** (at T-5 the canon faces may already be wired, in which case the ratio describes the migrated instance, not the legacy base T78 asked about). Lands in the deferred sizing pass with the rest of T78-am. **Sequencing: the build lands AFTER the after-set is shot** — not in Phase T, whose variable is a closed list of three under T81 with C43-am(3) forbidding anything joining afterwards; frozen in the interim per **T80-am2**. **FALSIFICATION PRE-COMMITTED, before the evidence was read (§1.5's discipline, and the reason it is stated here rather than after):** the after-set landed at `dd-import/tv-phase-t-after-2026-08-12/` while this ruling was being transcribed, and it **carries `clock-strings.tsv`** — the harness instrument owed since batch 34, now built. This ruling makes a checkable prediction about that file. At the moment of writing, **this seat has counted the set's files and opened none of them.** Pre-committed both ways: **within a group of equal-character-count clock strings in the right-anchored slot, the left ink edge MOVES** — that is what proportional digits mean and it is what T82 asserts; observing it **corroborates** T82 and T75-am3 and closes the `Clock` carve-out on rendered evidence as well as on the files. **If the left ink edge is instead INVARIANT within an equal-character-count group, T82's premise is wrong on the rendered surface** and this whole ruling re-opens — the atlas grant, the mandate's delivery clause and T75-am3's corrected disposition together — because the shipped stack would then be reaching a tabular advance by some path none of the three measurements found. Not a caveat: a stated way to be wrong, on evidence already on disk **T82-cl · batch 39 — THE FALSIFIER FIRED AND T82 SURVIVED IT.** Measured at this seat on the after-set's `clock-strings.tsv` plus its frames: 3-char `DD'` group **left-edge spread 22.0 px** where tabular predicts ~0; **correlation between ink width and the `hmtx` predicted advance +0.9824**; **residual sd about the fit 1.04 px**; narrowest `71'` 58.0 px to widest `90'` 80.0 px, **measured delta 22.0 px against 21.2 px predicted from the font's own advance table**; clock right edge spread 5 px, so the right-anchor holds. The left edge moves, and it moves by the amount the advance table predicts, to about a pixel. **A third instrument, sharing nothing with the two that produced the finding, in a third place.** Coverage stated (C28): 54 of 156 non-empty-clock frames measured, 37 in the clean subset, the remainder dropped where bloom merged the separator isolating the clock; the instrument's resolution is the 1.04 px residual, not the raw detection spread. **T75-am3's corrected disposition is confirmed on rendered evidence** — the left edge moves under the canon face, so the mandate is unmet by the STACK exactly as ruled, not by `Clock`'s face assignment **T82-b · batch 40 — the DERIVED FONT LANDED; source verified, and one line closes it.** `tnum` resolved at build time, **every digit exactly 1000 units, spread 0, cmap-only change** — the atlas route built exactly as granted: the font's own tabular glyphs, no outline invented, and a cmap-only diff is the narrowest possible expression of it. The unit is right, the change landing on the **asset** where T75-am put the property. **§2.5 holds the close open by one line** — *a source string is not a rendered element*; "every digit 1000 units, spread 0" measures the FILE, which is **necessary and not sufficient**, and is the same clause that made T82 a ruling rather than a note. **What closes it, with the instrument already built:** T75-am3's rendered line — within a set of equal-character-count clock strings in the right-anchored slot, **the left ink edge is INVARIANT**; the after-set already proved that instrument works by measuring the proportional case at 22.0 px spread on a 1.04 px residual, and the tabular case is the same measurement expecting ~0. **If TV's report carries that line, T82 CLOSES on this evidence; if not, one line is owed and nothing else.** **Still owed regardless, unchanged from the grant: the ASSET INVENTORY naming its members** — every generated asset any figure slot renders on, confirmed tabular, **including the condensed Bold 700 asset carrying `CashOut`**; a cmap-only change is per-file, three assets need three confirmations, and an inventory naming two of three is the defect C18 §4.1 exists for **T82-c · batch 41 — T82 does NOT close, and batch 40's close condition was this seat's to get wrong (§1.5).** TV's answer is correct and is the answer §2.5 was asking for: the derived font is proven tabular **at the font layer**, the shipped assets are **NOT wired to it** (held per the ruled sequencing). So **"mechanism accepted" matches the evidence and "the surface renders tabular" does not** — nothing on screen has changed, and **the rendered left-ink-edge line cannot run meaningfully until wiring** because there is nothing for it to measure. **Batch 40 wrote the close condition as though the assets were wired; they were not, precisely because this seat sequenced T82 after the pair and TV held that line.** The condition was not wrong about the INSTRUMENT, it was wrong about WHEN IT COULD RUN — the small version of the error C41 was written for, predicting a measurement against a state that does not exist yet. **State recorded precisely: mechanism GRANTED and verified at the font layer; surface UNCHANGED; the rendered line NOT OWED until wiring**, at which point it is one line on an instrument already built and already proven on the proportional case. **WIRING — GRANTED, and sequenced: after the sweep reports, not during.** One constant plus a regenerate; it waits on nothing else, the pair being shot and the freeze lifted. **It must not land mid-sweep** — G1-am4 ruled that a sweep running before the font lands has its digit-bearing rows voided, and landing the font INSIDE a running sweep does the same damage from the other direction, with this seat's instruction as the cause **T82-d · batch 42 — THE WIRING WORD: NOT YET, on exactly two conditions, both small and both already running.** **(1) `LegRowLine0`'s 1.8 px lands first** — wiring is otherwise a commit that CREATES a truncation on the leg statement, authorised by this seat, on a box the sweep named in advance, which is the one outcome the sweep existed to prevent. **(2) The six-slot enumeration completes** — an incomplete inventory cannot certify that LegRowLine0 is the ONLY wiring casualty, and a second one found after the commit is C18 §4.1 collecting on a debt this seat chose to run. **The principle behind the split, stated so it generalises: WIRING IS GATED ON THE FAILURES IT CREATES, NOT ON THE FAILURES THAT ALREADY EXIST.** `RiskPays` is 16.1 px over TODAY — that is T84's blocker, it already holds the ship, and it does not additionally hold the wiring; `LegRowLine0` passes today and fails on the commit, so it does. **On the word: send the enumeration result and LegRowLine0's relief together and the wiring word follows immediately** — it needs no further ruling, only those two facts; if the enumeration turns up further wiring casualties the same test applies to each, and none needs a fresh docket **T82-cl · batch 58 — CLOSED. The rendered half was already done, on the other case.** T82-c held the close open for the left-ink-edge line; TV delivered instead that **the sweep's tabular screen is a NO-OP on every digit-bearing row**, proportional and tabular measurements being identical on the wired tree. **Accepted, and stronger than what was asked — but only because the chain was already calibrated, which is the part worth writing down.** The left-ink-edge test measures the **consequence** (ink position) of equal advances; the screen measures **the advances themselves**, which is what the mandate is about. **§2.5 would normally hold this open** (a component measurement is not a frame) **and does not here, because T82-cl already measured the chain on the PROPORTIONAL case** — correlation **+0.9824** between measured ink width and the font's own advance table, residual **1.04px**, on this surface's own frames — so **the link from advance to rendered ink is MEASURED, not assumed**, and a component measurement of uniform advances lands on frames within about a pixel. **The wiring is confirmed by the same output:** the screen being a no-op means the derived font is **not merely present in the tree, it is what the surface renders.** **Residual risk named rather than waved:** digit-pair kerning could in principle move ink where advances are uniform, and T89-A's after-frames will corroborate incidentally — **corroboration, not a condition; the close does not wait on it.** **Endorsed as the best kind of instrument fix:** the sweep printed `no digits` both for a string with **no figures** and for one whose figures **screen to zero**, so `RISK $1,234  PAYS $12,340` was printing *no digits* — **the label was hiding the exact confirmation this condition asks for**, which is C18 §4.2's shape inside a log line | batch 38 |

The README's claim (*"Every moment matches on count and on seed. Nothing was selected, dropped or substituted"*) is **true as written and insufficient as a pairing claim** — nothing was substituted; the grammar simply is not one of the things the claim covers, and it moves pixels. **R43's mechanism one lane over: the seed pins the DEAL, not the timeline**, so the grammar occupying frame N is not pinned by pinning the run. **Exclusion AUTHORISED** under R23-am's test — named, measured, and resting on a real property of the subject rather than a slackened threshold; it is also **self-detecting, the discriminator being in the artifact's own filename** (C42's in-frame invariant in its cheapest possible form). **Two consequences that travel with the set: `g1-column-all-markets` has NO clean pair** (both frames mismatched), so any COMPARATIVE claim about that moment is single-sided — G1's verdict does not rest on it; and **`sat-down` retains one clean pair of five**. **On the 130 matched frames the migration is GRANTED:** renderer swapped `UI.Text` to TMP, the §4 canon faces landed, synthesised bold and italic gone — T81's closed variable, verified. **Not promoted to a law:** the general form (*a pairing claim names the variables it matched on, and the ones you pinned are not necessarily the ones that move*) sits close to R43's founding case without being identical; it promotes on a third catch | batch 39 |
| T84 | The extent-dependent layouts were never re-derived | **RULED — VIOLATION, BLOCKER; constitution §3.5, fourth instance and the first triggered by a FACE rather than a cap** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 39. Two violations, both on **clean grammar-matched pairs**, both new in the after half. **(a) The NEED line truncates to a fragment** — same seed, same ticket, same three legs: before renders `ONE TEAM BLANKED`, after renders **`ONE TEAM`**. That is not one of G1's authored forms; it is the word-boundary truncation backstop firing **on the line that tells the player what has to happen for his money to land, and it has dropped the operative word.** The deck exists so truncation is NEVER reached, and `ONE TEAM BLANKED` is itself the authored fallback — which overruns too. **S17's class: truncation that drops the rule is misleading at the point of spending.** **(b) The money control COLLIDES** — same seed, scene, grammar, moment and frame, a true pair: before renders `CASH OUT $131` then a clear gap then `HOLD E`; after renders **`CASH OUT $131OLD E`**, the label overprinted by the figure and spilling past its own field. §6.1's money control, and T63/T68/T68-am/T71's subject across four batches — T68 closed on *"the money control currently shows no instruction"*, and the instruction is now present and unreadable for a different reason. **Mechanism: the canon face is wider, so every layout whose bounds or whose neighbour's position was derived against the old face is now unverified.** **This is NOT T74 and must not be routed there** — T74 settles which size AUTHORITY governs (§4.1-ratio vs shipped-px) and a collision is not a size-authority question; this is **§3.5 in its own words** (*landing a cap is not landing the layout the cap implies; a bound added in one place obliges the layout depending on it to be re-derived in the same commit*), after T20, T47 and T51. **T81 does not cover it:** T81's *"point size is HELD, rendered EXTENT will move — that is the measurement, not a breach"* is correct, and **a string that overprints its neighbour is not a wider string, it is a broken layout**; T81's probe-is-an-instrument guard forbids tuning sizes INSIDE the pair, not fixing a collision after it. **RULED: the surface does not ship with a truncated NEED line or a collided money control — Phase T is NOT Design-verified until both clear.** The pair is unaffected: it is shot, the freeze is lifted, and these fixes land after it, which is where they belong; sequencing is the orchestrator's. **OWED — a SWEEP, not two fixes (C18 §4.1):** every slot whose extent, or whose neighbour's position, was derived against the pre-migration face, **inventory naming its members**. Two were found by eye in two of nine moments; **nobody has looked at the other seven, and this seat states that as its own blind spot rather than implying the list is two** **T84-am · batch 40 — the sweep's FIRST NAMED MEMBER: `MARKET SUSPENDED` overruns its own slot, SOLO.** A third instance and a **distinct sub-class**: T84's two founding cases were a truncation and a **collision between neighbours**; this is **one string too long for its own box with no neighbour involved** — **T20's clipping class**, returning in the wider canon face. Recorded as a **named member of T84's owed inventory**, not as a new item, because T84 owes a sweep whose members are named and this is the first the sweep produced. **Remedy class ruled now so the magnitude has somewhere to land:** the string is a state word in the cash-out slot and **TV-12/13 already grants it that slot exclusively** (*`MARKET SUSPENDED` owns the cash-out slot, no actionable offer beside it*), so **the span is available by PRIOR RULING** — span is the first route here rather than a request. Order unchanged: span, then size, **never the copy** (§4 / T24-am). Magnitude routes to T74 with the other tracking-0 survivors. **Not ruled here: the number** — that is the next docket **T84-am2 · batch 41 — TV's STRUCTURAL REFRAME accepted, and it is stronger than the sweep this seat asked for.** This seat asked for *every slot whose extent, or whose neighbour's position, was derived against the pre-migration face* — **a judgement call per slot**, made by whoever ran it. **TV found the structural fact that removes the judgement: the row builder FIXES ALL WIDTHS and never derives them from content, so EVERY reserved box carries an unstated fits-assumption sized against the NARROWER face**; the sweep now tests **every box against its longest renderable form**, tree-stamped per the directive. **Adopted as the sweep's definition — it converts a SAMPLE into a POPULATION** (C18 §4.1 satisfied properly rather than approximately) and it **retroactively explains T84's three founding instances: not three unlucky slots but three members of a class containing every fixed box on the surface**, surfacing first only because they were the ones somebody looked at. Promoted to a law at **C46**. **One binding condition on the digit-bearing boxes:** the assets are still wired to the PROPORTIONAL set (T82-c), but **tabular is what will ship and a tabular set is normalised toward the widest digit**, so for most strings the tabular worst case is **wider** — a sweep computing digit worst-cases against the currently-wired metrics **UNDERSTATES the requirement and can pass a box that fails the moment the font is wired**, which is the one direction of error that matters. **Ruled: the sweep computes its digit-bearing longest-renderable forms against the TABULAR metrics** — exact arithmetic, not an estimate, since every digit is exactly 1000 units and the cmap-only change leaves every non-digit advance untouched. **The screening may be computed; the boxes it flags take rendered confirmation**, which is where §2.5 binds — on the claims, not on the search | batch 39 |
| T85 | A fourth variable joined a closed list, and no document rules it | **RULED — the fold is a C43-am(3) breach; the value is UNRULED; remedy order set** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 39. The after-set names its variable as the type stack *"plus the rulings that ride with it — T73's real Condensed Bold 700, T77's italic struck, **T-5's tracking**"*. **`--tv-track-name` at .02 em is not in T81's variable** — T81 closed it at exactly three things and said *nothing joins later*: the renderer swap, the §4 canon face per slot, the retirement of synthesised styling. Tracking is none of the three. **C43-am's separability test decides against the fold: the face has NO null arm** (TMP renders from a baked named instance, so a face must be chosen) **while tracking HAS one** (`characterSpacing` 0) — it could have been sequenced out, so under C43-am(3) it should have been. **And it has no owner: `tv-design.md` contains NO tracking clause at all** — zero occurrences of tracking, character spacing or letter spacing anywhere in the owning document. The surface now carries a spacing value this seat never assigned: **T75's shape one property over**, a value rendering by defaulting rather than by ruling. **Material, not bookkeeping** — by the lane's own measurement it is **~10 px of the NEED overrun** at 18 characters, the overrun T84 and G1-am3 adjudicate. **Ruled remedy order: (1) remove the unruled tracking and RE-MEASURE** — a layout is not re-derived, a span is not widened and a ruled size is not shrunk to accommodate a parameter nobody ruled; **(2)** if the overrun survives at tracking 0, THEN span-or-size routed to T74 per G1-am; **(3)** the owning document **owes a tracking clause for this surface**, written at this seat against frames after T84 clears — **until it exists, .02 em is not canon and does not become canon by having shipped.** **The pair is NOT void** — the addition is uniform across the slots it touches and nameable in one line, which is C43-am(2)'s test — but **its stated variable is CORRECTED to include it and T81's closed list is recorded as breached**, because a closed list that absorbs a fourth item silently is not a closed list, and the next migration will read this row before it reads T81's **T85-am · batch 40 — the withdrawal's reason is NARROWED; `Label` .16 em and `Meta` .10 em STAND.** TV flagged both as resting on the same kit-not-owning-document authority as the withdrawn .02 em and **did not act** — the correct call, endorsed: a lead flagging an authority gap rather than resolving it by analogy is the contract working. **The analogy does NOT transfer, and the reason is the whole of T85:** .02 em was withdrawn because it was a **fourth variable inside an OPEN VERIFICATION PAIR** (C43-am(3)) — being unruled was the second half of that finding, not the whole of it. **.16 em and .10 em never joined Phase T's variable**; they predate it, they ship, and they sit in frames the studio has been reading for weeks. **Ruled: they STAND until the clause rules them** — removing them now would be an unruled change to the surface in the opposite direction on no evidence, the same defect the withdrawal corrected with the sign flipped. **An authority gap is not a licence to move a value; it is a reason to rule it.** **The owed clause's SCOPE is widened before it is written (C18 §4.1): it does not cover "these three" — it inventories EVERY slot on the surface carrying a tracking value, names each, and states the value and its basis**, because the three that surfaced are the three somebody happened to look at and the same kit-not-owning-doc basis plausibly covers more. T75 is the precedent — twelve slots rendering by defaulting, and the finding above the item was that half the surface's type had no owner. Written at this seat against frames after T84 clears; until it exists **none of the three is canon, and none becomes canon by having shipped** **T85-am2 · batch 42 — THE TRACKING CLAUSE IS RULED and lands in the owning document; `Label` .16 em and `Meta` .10 em RATIFIED; NEED stays 0 on DOCTRINE.** **The doctrine is not new — it is the laptop's, applied:** `surething-design.md` §4.3 already rules that **short uppercase labels are tracked, factual copy stays literal, and a label-plus-instruction is factual copy** (S68 is the worked case; S70 added the exception shape — OS chrome takes its own token, *a named exception with one member is still named*). **That is a VOICE principle, not a surface-specific one, and it transfers: tracking marks a LABEL, never a FACT.** A tracked label says *this is furniture, read past it*; tracking a fact spaces out the thing the player is meant to read. **Ruled: `Label` .16 em RATIFIED as built** (label-class slot, tracked as the doctrine wants); **`Meta` .10 em RATIFIED as built** (same class, lighter value for a smaller quieter slot); **`NEED` .02 em stays at 0, now on doctrine and not only on procedure.** **That last is the substantive half:** T85 withdrew .02 em as a fourth variable inside an open pair, a PROCEDURAL finding that would have let the value return once the pair closed — **it does not return, because NEED is the requirement statement, the single most factual string on the surface, and tracking it is tracking a fact.** The value that broke the procedure was also the only one of the three that breaks the doctrine, and that convergence is why this is rulable now rather than waiting on the inventory. Both ratifications rest on frames the studio has read for weeks — **C23's shape**: the build's value is the one seen at review distance, the document's has never existed. **The clause lands in `tv-design.md` §4 with its INVENTORY OPEN and saying so** — doctrine, the three ruled members, and an explicit statement that it is incomplete pending the six unswept slots. **An honestly-bounded clause beats no clause and the constitution says so** (§1.1 — a stub is legitimate; an empty section pretending to authority is the failure). **The doctrine binds NOW**, which is the point: without it the six slots still being enumerated can acquire unruled tracking while everyone waits for a complete list. Until a slot is named in that clause its value is not canon and does not become canon by having shipped **T85-am3 · batch 43 — the six proposed slots: THREE RATIFIED, ONE REFUSED, TWO HELD for their strings.** **RATIFIED as proposed — `TakeoverTitle`, `TakeoverSub`, `Subtitle` → 0:** TV's rationale carries it and is exactly the doctrine, *they carry money and leg facts*; doubly right for anything carrying a money figure, since a tracked tabular figure would space out the one thing the mandate exists to hold still. Ratified on the RATIONALE, not on the slot names. **REFUSED — `Consolation` → Label; it takes 0.** TV's reason is *"authored voice"* and that is precisely why: **the doctrine's split is not match-fact versus product-voice, it is LABEL versus COPY** — a label is furniture you read past, copy is written to be read, and **a consolation line is a written line**. `surething-design.md` §4.3 is explicit and this is its case (*factual copy stays literal; a label-plus-instruction is factual copy*); authored narration sits on the copy side. **T77 already ruled the remedy for this exact slot** — *size, value or position carry it, never a letterform the family does not have* — and tracking is the same move T77 refused, one channel over. **HELD, not refused — `InterventionPrompt` and `Attract`:** both proposed Label on *"control affordances / instructional prompts"*, and both are unrulable from a slot name. **S68 is the founding case for exactly this error** — a tracking value applied by class to strings whose class differed from what the slot suggested, costing a batch and 38 px of headroom. Two specific doubts, each from a prior ruling: **`Attract`** — T27 ruled the idle copy prints `ROUND n OF 8 · BOARD OPEN` in `--tv-fact`, so if that is this slot's string it is **a fact, in the fact ink, by prior ruling, and it carries a digit**, which is a 0; **`InterventionPrompt`** — §4.3 splits the very class cited, a short affordance name (`HOLD E` shape) being a label while **a label-plus-instruction is factual copy**, and which side it lands on is a property of the string. **Cheap to resolve — the string-set enumeration that just completed is the artifact that answers it.** **DISPOSITIONS PRE-COMMITTED so neither needs a second round: a short name or affordance (one or two tokens, no instruction, no fact) → Label as proposed; a string carrying a fact, a figure, or an instruction in sentence form → 0.** **Standing practice added to the clause: A SLOT IS CLASSIFIED AGAINST ITS STRINGS, NOT ITS NAME** — a slot's name describes its position, only its strings reveal its voice; two catches on two surfaces (S68, and two of six here) is enough to write it beside the rule it protects rather than leave it as advice **T85-am4 · batch 44 — BOTH HELD SLOTS TAKE 0; branch 2 fires on both; THE CLAUSE CLOSES.** **`InterventionPrompt` → 0**, one site: `SHOT FROZEN` plus `[M] MULLIGAN · [R] SEND TO REVIEW (99%) · [N] LET IT DIE` — **instruction form carrying a figure**, branch 2 exactly as pre-committed; the slot renders both strings so the stricter governs. **`Attract` → 0**, four sites: `SIT TO WATCH THE SWEAT` is **an instruction in sentence form** (branch 2) and three of the four are sentences; `SHOP OPEN` alone would have been a Label and does not get its own value, because the stricter string governs the slot. **This seat's stated REASON for doubting `Attract` was WRONG and the branch fired anyway (§1.5):** the prediction was that it carried T27's `ROUND n OF 8 · BOARD OPEN`, a fact in the fact ink with a digit — it carries none of that, and the disposition holds on a different string for a different reason, which is what a pre-commitment written against a TEST rather than a GUESS is supposed to do. Recorded because a pre-commitment landing for the wrong reason deserves the same scrutiny as one that misses. **THE CLAUSE CLOSES, and closes TOTALLY rather than by exhaustion:** nine slots now carry a ruled value while **§4 names 23 slot types**, so fourteen have no entry — **which is exactly the state T75 caught, defaulted rather than ruled.** **Ruled: every slot not named in the clause's table is 0, BY RULE and not by default** — tracking is opt-in and named, and silence becomes a ruling rather than a gap. the principle underneath it is RULED GENERAL.** TV reports `(99%)` is **the same quantity class as T16's subject at a different site** — a leg's probability at a decision prompt rather than on the momentum tape — and asks whether T16 reaches beyond the tape. **It does, but *does ruling X reach site Y* is exactly the question S67 was written about** (*a ruling can only reach the call sites that route through the thing it was ruled on*); nothing existing may be narrowed to exclude it** | batch 49 |

no timer, no auto-commit*), and the mechanic earns it: **a frozen shot that requires walking to the laptop is not a frozen shot.** **(b) EVERY SPENDING OPTION TAKES T22's FALLBACK GESTURE, UNCHANGED** — hold to preview, the preview showing what the option does **and what it costs** (T86-am: the basis for a decision is an offer, not an opinion); ruled as the general shape, **the weight of the gesture matches the weight of the act**, which also stops the three reading as peers when two spend and one does not. **(d) Until the gesture is built the copy must NOT say `HOLD`** — a label describing a safety the input lacks is the defect, **but shipping the honest label on a press-to-commit control is worse than either, so the fix is the GESTURE and not the word**;
