| V6 | The room-region gate reads a different quantity than its bounds describe | **RULED — room's recommendation ADOPTED: V6 measures in CIELAB via the room's shared `linear_to_lab`; the bounds stay exactly as ruled, 85–92°** · DD 2026-08-11 batch 37, on room's provenance trace (`6e61c9d`). New row — V6 has been referenced from T65 since batch 13 and never had one. Provenance ACCEPTED in full: traced three independent ways (vocabulary — the room pairs hue with CHROMA, never *saturation*; instrument — the lane's only hue tool is `linear_to_lab`, and the single HSV hit in room code is `v6_room_region.py` itself, added by TV at `97350ae`; computation — CIELAB lands within 1.9° on the lid and 10.7° on the key while HSV misses by 39–51°). **Why the READING moves and not the bounds:** the bounds ARE the studio's palette figures (lid 85.1–85.3°, warm key ~92°, both entering at batch 13 as this lane's CIELAB measurements); re-deriving them in HSV would keep the code but orphan the numbers T65 clause 4 cites and leave every room palette figure in the studio permanently incomparable with V6's output — under C33-am3 (*the three ladders are never compared*) a gate whose output cannot be compared with the palette it polices is a STANDING defect, not a local one. **Naming the space is necessary and NOT sufficient — do both.** Room is exactly right that a label makes the mismatch visible without making the bounds correct; the asymmetry inside the file is the tell (line 91 names its luma unit per C33, the hue line names nothing) and **that asymmetry is where the error entered**, so leaving it after a conversion invites the next one. **EVERY historical V6 verdict is VOID — BOTH directions.** Room's self-test settles it: feed the band the two colours it was derived from and **both fail it** — a gate whose own reference points fall outside its own band is not mis-calibrated, it is reading a different quantity. The regions are **DISJOINT, not shifted** (HSV 85–92° ≈ CIELAB 124–128°, green), so in-band verdicts certify nothing AND out-of-band verdicts are equally void — a rejected value may have been a correct warm one. TV's "every in-band verdict is suspect" is right in direction and understates it. **This recovers work as often as it discards it** (C37's shape, and why the ruling is *void* rather than *failed*). **TV OWES an inventory of V6 verdicts already acted on, both directions** (C18 §4.1 — an inventory names its members); room correctly disclaims this as TV's record, and "we'll re-run it" is not the deliverable, the members are. **Ownership (C20, both lanes present):** `v6_room_region.py` is TV's, `linear_to_lab` is room's and already shared by the emission instrument and the R23 cast — **the converter is SHARED, NEVER FORKED**, because a second implementation is exactly how a two-space defect regrows and it would be invisible to the same self-test that caught this one. **The amplitude window is NOT independent evidence:** `[0.78, 1.06]` and the *"130° at zero falling to ~45.5°"* trajectory sit in the same source comment as the HSV 88.0° and are functions of the band — **both VOID as evidence, re-derived against the converted gate**; they bound nothing today and anyone citing them is citing an HSV derivation of a CIELAB constraint. **NOT FROZEN, and that is the useful sequencing:** T80-am froze T65's re-authored VALUE because it moves rendered pixels inside the Phase T pair, but V6 is an INSTRUMENT — converting its reading changes no rendered frame, so no freeze attaches. **Repair the gate during the freeze**, so that when the freeze lifts T65's value is judged by an instrument that works; lifting the freeze first and measuring against the broken gate spends the after-set on a verdict void on arrival | batch 37 |

Third fault-between-seats instance (with R40's form and C13) | batch 20 |
| C40 | A rule states the property it protects; a proxy is labelled as one | **Law** · DD 2026-08-09 batch 28: where a ruling names a detectable test alongside the property it protects, the test is labelled as the PROXY and the property as the LAW — so a passing proxy is never read as the property held (the S71-am2 false positive is the founding case: a word-count proxy read as a voice law) | batch 28 |
| C41 | A prediction from a contaminated frame is a floor, not a target | **Law** · DD 2026-08-10 batch 29, **APPROVED — Allen, same day; constitution §4.6**: where an expected value derives from a frame that still contains the element under test, it is a FLOOR stated as a direction of travel — never a number to land on. Founding case: batch 27's 6.47/6.58 'pre-verified' band — _goldFlood at alpha 0 still fed 0.0269 of ground (an element at zero alpha is not an absent element; §2.5's shape one step earlier). Landing INSIDE the band would have meant residue survived — the pre-commitment had inverted its own test. Pre-commitment NOT weakened: expressing it as a VALUE is what failed; 'ground falls, ink stops moving' would have been satisfied exactly | batch 29 |
| C42 | A confound is separated by an invariant in the same frame, not by a bound on its size | **Law (register-level; deliberately NOT proposed for the constitution — one founding case, C39's precedent)** · DD 2026-08-10 batch 31: a bound is a standing claim about runs nobody has taken (batch 29's needed an n it never had); an in-frame invariant re-establishes itself every measurement. Founding case: the pinned re-shoot's row-number group (moved 0.0002, content-fixed) isolating the season-record 0.0247 as deal variation in one frame. C36 is this principle's temporal form; C42 its population form | batch 31 |
| C43 | A migration moves one variable, because its verification pair is the instrument | **Law (register-level, C39/C42's precedent — promotes on a second catch)** · DD 2026-08-11 batch 32: a migration's before/after pair is only an instrument if the migration is the ONLY moved variable; folding a second change (a size re-derivation, a weight fix) into it destroys the pair's power to certify either. Founding case: T74 — the size-authority conflict must not be settled silently inside Phase T. *(Row moved from the TV table to Cross-surface 2026-08-11 — factual bookkeeping, not an amendment: a cross-surface law transcribed into a surface's table is invisible to anyone reading the laws end-to-end, and C43 is now load-bearing in three items (T74, T79, T80). ID unchanged per C22.1; the ruling is untouched. Section counts move TV 86→85, Cross-surface 41→42; total unchanged at 265.)* **Cause, recorded at the authoring end (§1.5 — this seat's, not the transcriber's):** batch 32 placed C43 as its final section after T72–T78 and closed with the single instruction *"transcribe T72–T78 and C43 into the tables"* — a cross-surface law bundled into a TV-item list with **no destination table named**. Transcribing in that reading order lands it after the T-block, which is what happened; the transcription followed the instruction as written, which is exactly the case §1.5 protects. **Standing practice, at authoring not transcription: every batch names the destination table for every row it ships**, because the authoring seat is where that information exists. Binds this seat too — the DD has transcribed its own rows directly since batch 20 | batch 32 **C43-am · batch 35 — ONE VARIABLE MEANS ONE *SEPARABLE* VARIABLE (first live application, ruled at T81).** C43's named examples — a size re-derivation, a weight fix — are changes that COULD have been sequenced out, and T79 was, on exactly that ground. This adds the test for when they cannot be. **Two changes are ONE variable when neither can be performed without the other. The test is SEPARABILITY, not size:** if arm A cannot be executed without also landing some value of B, B is not a fold — it is a parameter of A, and the pair is honest at the resolution of both. **Three corollaries, all exercised at T81:** (1) **check for a null arm first** — where a migration's target medium REQUIRES a value the source medium supplied implicitly, there is no "leave it alone" arm, only a choice of value; preserving the legacy value is itself a change, usually to a third value matching neither; (2) **a pair whose blind spot cannot be NAMED loses to one whose can** (C18 §4.2) — a change that isolates unevenly across the surface, with no in-frame marker of where it isolated, is a worse instrument than a uniformly coarser pair that states its resolution in one line; (3) **coarsening is bounded at authoring** — a pair ruled coarse names a CLOSED list of what its variable contains, everything else stays sequenced out, and nothing joins the list afterwards. **The precedence C43 does not override:** where a surface's owning document has already ruled the value a migration must land, or has already assigned the migration's pair a question, C43 does NOT license shipping a non-canon set to protect the pair — §1.2 puts the owning document above the register's ruling for the item on that surface's content. **Not promoted to the constitution:** still register-level; this seat does not promote its own law inside the batch that first applies it |
| C25 | Instrument scope is part of a measurement | **Law** · DD 2026-08-03: a measurement is reported with its scope attached, in the same breath, unprompted — what it reads AND what it cannot see. Promoted from the markets lead's PlayMode margin-invariant note (best-formed C18 §4.2 instance the studio has produced); recorded as the pattern to copy | batch 8 *(Row moved from the Phone table to Cross-surface 2026-08-11 batch 35 — bookkeeping, ID unchanged per C22.1; a cross-surface instrument law parked in a surface's table is invisible to anyone reading the laws end-to-end, and this one belongs beside the instrument family C33/C36/C37/C41.)* |
| C26 | Owning documents owed | **Open, sequenced** · DD 2026-08-03: SureThing's and TV's owning docs do not exist (room's approved R13; phone stub by design). Writing one against an unsettled surface produces another `08`. Sequence: SureThing's after LEDGER close-out + S48 fold; TV's after T41/T48/T49. Neither blocks; both owed before either surface is called finished | batch 8 **C26-am3 · batch 20 — the phone's stub expires (phone only; other surfaces stay closed):** the phone and laptop speak in one glance with two voices (lowercase sentence-case in a non-studio face vs uppercase Archivo; ~85% empty; header hue unmeasured against a retired-hue register). The phone's owning doc is OWED, written at the DD seat, sequenced AFTER the phone has a reference frame. R28-am untouched (live engine data only — the owed thing is treatment, which never had an owner). Reason restated after the blur fix: it was never about sharpness, and a clear screen shows its treatment more, not less *(Row moved from the Phone table to Cross-surface 2026-08-11 batch 35 — bookkeeping, ID unchanged per C22.1; it governs all four surfaces' owning docs and only its `-am3` is phone-specific.)* |
| C44 | A bound and the reading it judges must come from one instrument | **Law (register-level, C43's standing — not a constitutional amendment)** · DD 2026-08-11 batch 37: **a bound and the reading compared against it must be produced by the same instrument, in the same stated space, and a gate demonstrates this by printing both.** *Founding case:* **V6** — bound in CIELAB, reading in HSV, the two regions **DISJOINT**, and both of the gate's own founding colours failing its own band; every verdict it ever issued was void in both directions and nothing in the pipeline was positioned to notice. *Second and third catches, inside one fortnight:* this seat's own batch-36 T65 target (an authored value ruled against a rendered band — T65-am2), and the room lane's twice-paid region-comparison error, which room names in its own T65 record. **Relation to C33-am3:** that clause requires every measurement to state its space and it governed REVIEW PROSE — **V6 stated nothing and nothing required it to**, because the clause never reached a gate's own output, which is where the comparison actually happens. C44 puts it there. **The cheap test, promoted from room's own method: feed a bound the values it was derived from; if they fail, the gate is reading a different quantity than its bounds describe.** It costs one run, needs no frames, and is the only check in this family that catches a **disjoint** band rather than a merely loose one — a shifted band still passes its own founding values, a disjoint one cannot. **Standing practice: any gate carrying a numeric bound runs its founding values through itself once, and records the result beside the bound** **V6-am · batch 51 — THE BAND'S TOP IS WITHDRAWN, and this seat installed it wrongly (§1.5); V6's BOUNDS are void with it.** Room's R23 pair (`38c44da`) fired **disposition 3**: the anchors do not separate — **the high anchor reads 101.4 against a recorded ~92, a 9.4° miss on a 7° band** — and the subject was correctly **not reported as conforming**. **The find is decisive: two independent computations agree and both disagree with the record** — the key tube's **authored** colour computes **102.7°**, its **rendered** contribution measures **101.4°**, they agree within **1.3°**, and the recorded top carries **no stated derivation at all**. **That is C44's founding shape applied to a BAND rather than a gate: feed a bound its own founding value and it fails.** **§1.5 — THE NUMBER WAS ON THE RECORD AT BATCH 37, INSIDE THE TRACE THIS SEAT ACCEPTED IN FULL.** V6's own row reads *computation — CIELAB lands within 1.9° on the lid and **10.7° on the key***, and **92 + 10.7 = 102.7, exactly the authored figure measured today**. This seat **read a 10.7° miss on a 7°-wide band as CONFIRMATION that the band was correctly drawn** — a miss larger than the band is not agreement, it is the anchor saying it is not there. **Two errors, one cause:** those gaps were also re-labelled *the authored↔rendered gap*, which they never were — they are **record↔computation** gaps, the true authored↔rendered gap on the key being **1.3°**. Both mistakes are the same act, **attributing a discrepancy to the wrong pair of quantities — exactly what C44 was written to prevent, one batch after this seat wrote it.** **THE THREE-BATCH CHAIN, RECORDED STRAIGHT: batch 36's INSTINCT WAS RIGHT and its remedy wrong** — it restated the band to 83–85° reasoning that *the upper half brackets no measured member*, and the upper half indeed bracketed none, **because the real member sits ABOVE the band rather than below its top**; **batch 37 rejected 36's remedy correctly and then installed a provenance that did not exist**, restoring a top the trace in front of it was contradicting. **Batch 36 deserves the credit its correction took away — it detected the defect.** **RULED: the recorded ~92 is WITHDRAWN, and this seat DECLINES to install 101.4 in its place** — because **the choice of the key tube as the upper anchor is itself underived**, having entered the same way ~92 did, and moving the number without deriving the membership **repeats the exact defect one number later**, leaving the register carrying its third unsourced band top in three batches. **THE BAND IS RE-DERIVED ONCE, and the derivation states: (1) every MEMBER NAMED** (C18 §4.1) with its value, its **space** (CIELAB on linear, C33-am3), whether it is **authored or rendered**, and the surface or frame it was taken on; **(2) whether the key tube and the screen emitters are ONE POPULATION AT ALL** — the screens cluster tightly (lid 85.1–85.3, phone 85.4, laptop 84.3, a representative warm emission 83.3) while **the key sits ~16° above all of them**, and **a band spanning a tight cluster plus one distant member is either two populations or a bound so loose it certifies nothing**, which changes every verdict the band will ever issue; **(3) C44's OWN PRACTICE run and recorded beside the bound** — the founding values through the band once — **which had it run when C44 was ruled would have surfaced this immediately, V6 being C44's own founding case**, so the practice was written and not applied to the gate that produced it (recorded as a process finding, not a new law). **DIRECTION OFFERED, EXPLICITLY NOT A RULING:** the settlement re-tint is a **light event**, so the comparison class is the room's warm **LIGHT**, which is the key — the screens being objects lit by it rather than members of the same population; if that holds, a light event is judged against lights and the screen cluster is a different band answering a different question. **The derivation decides this; this seat is naming the fork, not taking it.** **V6's OWN BOUNDS ARE VOID WITH THE TOP:** V6 was repaired by converting its READING to CIELAB while its **bounds stayed 85–92**, so **that repair is incomplete and V6 is still mis-calibrated, now in a new way** — it takes the re-derived band before issuing another verdict; **its converted reading stands, only the bound moves** **V6-am2 · batch 52 — THE BAND, RE-DERIVED. The fork is answered: they are NOT one population and never were.** **Every member named, rendered CIELAB on linear (C33-am3), from the register's own rows:** `ArtIndicator` **49.7°** chroma 5.4 (R41 b16–17, standby lamp under **room-rust law**); `WindowGlow` **77.0°** (R42 b15, **textured emitter — the map governs**); representative warm emission **83.3°** (T65-am); laptop **84.3°**/5.3 and phone **85.4°**/5.0 (R39 b15); laptop lid **85.1–85.3°** (S63-am2 b13); **warm key tube 101.4° rendered / 102.7° authored** (room, `38c44da`). **THE ORIGIN OF ~92 IS NOW VISIBLE IN THE RECORD:** S63-am2, batch 13, reads *room cast 355.7°→85.1° **vs the key's 92°*** — **a passing comparison inside a note about the laptop lid**, never a derivation, never a measurement of the key, never given a space, **which then became a band edge by being cited. That is the whole provenance, and it is why it had none.** **THE FORK, ANSWERED: the spread is 49.7° to 101.4° — 52° wide — which is not a family but FOUR KINDS OF OBJECT measured with one instrument.** **The screens are one family and a tight one** (83.3 / 84.3 / 85.1–85.3 / 85.4, about **2° wide**) and tight **by construction**, R39 having closed them on a **shared base** (`Amp(1/3/15)` off `LaptopScreen.GrantedLidEmission`) — they are unified because one constant makes them so. **`WindowGlow` is a PICTURE, not a palette member** — R42 ruled the authored value a **multiplier** with the **emission map governing**, so its 77.0° is the night-city sodium texture and not a colour anyone chose; **nothing about it is authored as a hue, so it cannot be a band member.** **`ArtIndicator` is ratified rust law** at 49.7°. **And the key tube is the room's LIGHT** — every other member is an object IN the room; the key is what lights them. **RULED: the 83–85° band is the SCREENS' family — correct, well-founded, and the key tube was NEVER a member of it. The phantom top was A LIGHT SOURCE APPENDED TO A FAMILY OF SCREENS**, a category error, and **the same error this lane has now paid for three times in other forms** (authored-vs-rendered, HSV-vs-CIELAB, record-vs-computation) — **comparing across populations is this lane's signature defect and it produced the band's top.** **WHICH POPULATION THE SUBJECT BELONGS TO:** `RoomSettlementGlow()` paints **light into the room** — not a screen, not a texture, not an object — so **its comparison class is the room's warm LIGHT, the key.** **Consequence: the subject was never in the screens' band's jurisdiction at all**, and every verdict judging it against 83–85 or 85–92 was **judging a light event against a family of screens.** **THE BOUND: reference SOURCED, tolerance OWED, and not invented here.** The reference is **the key's rendered 101.4°**, sourced two independent ways agreeing within 1.3° — unlike `~92`. **A one-member population gives a POINT, not a band**, so a tolerance is required and has no derivation yet; **its BASIS is ruled and its NUMBER is not: floor = the instrument's demonstrated spread on this subject**, since a bound tighter than the instrument's own spread cannot fail for what it exists to catch (**C32's founding shape**). **Room reported that spread as *77% of band* — a percentage of a band that is now void — and it is needed IN DEGREES: a restatement of data already in hand, NOT a capture, no window required.** Anything tighter than that floor needs a **perceptual** basis nobody has measured, and **inventing one would be the third unsourced number on this band in four batches** **V6-am3 · batch 53 — THE GATE CHECKS A VALUE AGAINST ITS OWN FIXTURE FAMILY, NOT AGAINST ONE ROOM-WIDE BAND.** A single band across four fixture families was always going to be **either too loose to catch anything or wrong for three of them — and it was both.** **Ruled: V6 checks each value against its own family's bound**, the families being named at T65-cl with their values and provenance; **a value belonging to NO family is not gate-able by a family bound** and is ruled at this seat against the property, which is what T65-cl did. **STANDING, promoted from room's line because it is the general form: A BOUND IS DEFENDED BY THE REASONING THAT GROUPS ITS MEMBERS, NEVER BY HOW TIGHTLY THEY HAPPEN TO CLUSTER.** Clustering is evidence that a grouping **might** exist; it is never the grouping's justification. **Four batches on this band, and every wrong turn — the phantom top, the lights-vs-screens carve, the cross-population comparisons — came from treating PROXIMITY as MEMBERSHIP** | batch 37 |
| C45 | A freeze is checked against the seat's own OUTPUT, not only the lane's input | **Law (register-level, C39/C42/C43's standing — not proposed for the constitution)** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 38: where a freeze protects a verification pair, a routed **measurement** respects it by construction — measurements move no pixels and a lane reporting one is correctly freeze-clean. **The RULING that measurement produces may not be.** The freeze check therefore runs **at the moment of ruling, on what the ruling causes to change**, and **it is this seat's to run because the lane cannot see it**: from inside the lane the submission is freeze-clean and stays that way right up until someone rules on it. **Two founding cases, one batch apart, two different lanes:** **T80-am** — room's T65 settlement-cast measurement, where room stated the freeze was respected and that was true of a measurement and stopped being true when the measurement became a ruling that re-authored a value inside the pair's own frames; and **T82 / T80-am2** — TV's tabular measurement, identical shape, freeze-clean as a finding and a frozen variable the moment option (a) was granted. **Standing practice: any ruling issued while a verification pair is open states, IN THE RULING, whether it creates a frozen variable.** Two independent founding cases is what makes this a law at all; a third argues for promotion to constitution §4 beside the other instrument clauses | batch 38 |
| C46 | A fixed box carries an unstated assumption about the face it was sized against | **Law (register-level, C39/C42/C43's standing — not proposed for the constitution)** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 41, promoted from TV's structural reading of T84's sweep. **Where a layout FIXES a box's width rather than deriving it from content, that box carries an implicit claim — that the longest string it can hold fits, IN THE FACE IT WAS SIZED AGAINST. The claim is never written down, and a change of face, weight, tracking or figure set invalidates every such box at once: silently, and everywhere, because nothing in the box refers to the face it assumed.** Two consequences, both exercised: **(1) sweep the POPULATION, not the suspects** — after any change to type metrics the set at risk is *every fixed box*, not the ones a reader can think of; T84's three founding instances were found by eye in two of nine moments, and the structural reading found the class. **(2) The test is the longest RENDERABLE form, not the current content** — a box passes on what is in it today and fails on the string nobody captured. **Founding case:** Phase T, where a wider canon face produced a truncation (`ONE TEAM BLANKED` to `ONE TEAM`), a collision (`CASH OUT $131OLD E`) and a solo clipping (`MARKET SUSPENDED`) from **the same unstated assumption in three unrelated boxes**. **Retrospective fit, named as retrospective and NOT counted as founding:** S68 on the laptop, the SKIP ROUND headroom collapsing 45 px to 7 px when tracking landed at the wrong value — same shape, a fixed box and a metric change, one surface over. **Relation to the neighbours:** constitution §3.5 says *a bound is not a layout*, obliging the dependent layout to be re-derived; **C46 is its converse and its harder half — a fixed box IS a bound, an implicit one, and nobody re-derives what they never noticed they declared.** R30's *a locked band is not headroom* is the same family and already binds all three surfaces' fixed canvases, which is why this sits cross-surface rather than in TV's table. **Promotes to the constitution on a second INDEPENDENT founding case**; this seat is not counting a retrospective attribution as one **C46-am · batch 56 — A BOX SIZED BEFORE A LAW CANNOT SATISFY IT BY ACCIDENT.** C46 ruled that a fixed box carries an unstated assumption about **the face it was sized against**; the same box carries a second, **about the SET OF OBLIGATIONS its control had when the box was drawn.** **Every correct ruling that adds a required element to a control — a cost, a contract, an instruction, a state — makes that control's box worse**, and four consecutive rulings did it on one surface. **This is not evidence the rulings are wrong; it is the boxes encoding an older contract.** **Consequence, worth having in advance: when a ruling adds an element to a control, the box's fit is RE-DERIVED IN THE SAME BREATH AS THE RULING** — not discovered by a sweep two batches later — and the ruling **states whether it expects the box to still hold, saying so where it cannot tell.** **And the remedy is usually COMPOSITION, not span:** a control that has acquired a fourth obligation is rarely fixed by widening, but by admitting it now has more than one row's worth of things to say — **three instances on one surface in one pass** (the intervention prompt, `TakeoverSub`, the money control), all answered by **N independent strings are not one row** **C46-am2 · batch 58 — THE TWO SWEEP DIRECTIONS ARE COUPLED, and this is TV's finding.** T89-C required both directions on the reasoning that *nothing about the over-generation catch bounds the opposite direction*; **TV found something better — they are not independent.** **An over-generated string is only harmless when it is NOT the maximum. When it IS the maximum it sets the certified worst case, every producible form beneath it is never reached, and it therefore CONCEALS exactly the under-generation the other direction is looking for — so the two directions cannot be swept separately, and finding one does not clear the other.** **Founding case, both halves on one slot:** `BRICKLAYERS ANYTIME` was unproducible **and** the widest member of its set, so removing it did not merely delete a phantom — **it UNCOVERED that both generated arms had been swept against a hand-picked champion**, and that `LegRowNeed0` had been certified **17.5px BETTER than it is** (272.4 → 289.9). **That direction is invisible in frames by construction**, which is why it was the condition this seat cared most about, and **it fired.** **Two further shapes from the same report: the CODE KNEW the worst case and the sweep did not use it** — the construction site's own comment names `GRAVEDIGGERS TO WIN` as over budget, and **a worst case documented in a comment beside the generator is not a worst case the generator uses**; and **a form from the NEIGHBOURING SLOT had been certified against this one's box** (`MIDDLEMEN ML` in `LegRowNeed0`, where `ML` is the LINE slot's vocabulary), so **a worst case must come from the slot's OWN vocabulary, not from a plausible string.** **The remedy is the durable part and is adopted as the standard: CHAMPIONS ARE RETIRED** — the sweep **generates every producible form over the closed pools** (20 club nouns, 12 surnames, both arms each, plus authored constants and both fallbacks verbatim from their construction sites), so **a name added to either pool can no longer be missed by a champion nobody re-picked** — R16's hard-coded list of five materials, one instrument over | batch 41 |
| C47 | The match has three outcomes; a bet has two | **Law, cross-surface** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 49. Sited here rather than in a surface table because it governs **both owning documents' settlement language**, and a cross-surface law transcribed into a surface's table is invisible to anyone reading the laws end-to-end — **C43's founding defect, now seen twice.** **A draw is a third MATCH outcome. It is NOT a third BET outcome. Result language needs no third word: a leg either landed or it did not.** **Ruled: back the draw and it draws — you WON; back a team and it draws — you LOST.** Two words still cover every bet that can be placed, and **S23's enum (PENDING · RIDING · LIVE · GREEN · DEAD · VOID · CASHED OUT) is UNCHANGED and needs nothing added.** **What this prevents, and why it is a law rather than an answer: inventing a `DREW` leg state would be MODELLING THE MATCH INSIDE THE BET'S VOCABULARY** — a category error, and **the same one `Side` already refuses in the engine.** A leg has no opinion about how the match ended; it knows only whether its condition held. **The one legitimate neighbour, named so it is not mistaken for a counter-example:** a market that RETURNS THE STAKE on a draw (draw-no-bet's family) is a **VOID**, which the enum already carries — that is a different market's rule, never a third result. **Laptop echo, ruled by the same line: slip settlement language does not change** — no new word, no new state, no new column | batch 49 |
| C48 | A control's copy is its input contract | **Law, cross-surface** · DD 2026-08-12 batch 50, promoted from T88 and extending T59 to the gesture axis. **Where a control's label names a GESTURE, that gesture is what the input implements — the label is not a description of the control, it is the contract the player acts on.** T59 ruled the availability axis (*display state and input state are the same state, read from one value*); **this is the same law where the input is not merely available or refused but SHAPED** — held, pressed, released. **A label naming a SAFETY PROPERTY the input does not provide is the most dangerous copy a control can carry**, because it is relied upon precisely when the player is being careless, and because the reliance is invisible until the moment it costs him. **Founding case:** the TV's intervention prompt printing `HOLD` over a `wasPressedThisFrame` input that **committed an irreversible spend on the first frame** — no preview, no abandon path, on a surface the player is watching rather than operating (T88). **Corollary, ruled with it: the weight of the gesture matches the weight of the act.** An irreversible spend is never one frame of input; an action that costs nothing and is already the default may be. **The repair direction is fixed and it is the non-obvious half:** where copy and input disagree on a money control, **the INPUT is corrected to match the copy, not the copy to match the input** — correcting the word ships an honest description of an unsafe control, which is worse than either half alone. **Binds all four surfaces**, every one of which has controls whose labels name gestures | batch 50 |
| C49 | Money the player can win or lose is printed in FULL | **Law, cross-surface** · DD 2026-08-13 batch 57, the abbreviation ruling owed at T74-am3. Sited cross-surface because **money precision is a PRODUCT property, not a surface one** — the laptop's ledger, tallies and verdict figures are governed by the same sentence as the theatre's payout. **A money figure the player can win, lose, stake or be paid is printed in full: every digit, no abbreviation, no rounding, no `k`, no `M`, no cap.** **Three refusals, each on its own ground rather than on taste: (1) abbreviation discards precision on the one fact his money depends on, and it does so ONLY FOR LARGE NUMBERS — so it degrades exactly the wins that matter most**; `PAYS $12.3k` is not a payout, it is a description of one. **(2) A format that changes with magnitude is a twitch of a different kind:** this surface has a tabular mandate precisely so figures stop moving as they change, and a rendering that switches form at `$9,999 → $10.0k` **re-introduces the instability from the other end, after T82 paid to remove it.** **(3) A cap is a lie about money** — displaying less than he wins is a false factual claim, and a price or a payout is a fact; **C19's *deliberate cap that prints its own count* governs LISTS, never AMOUNTS.** **Also refused and already ruled elsewhere: the figure does not SHRINK** — `tv-design.md` §8's *copy truncates or chooses a shorter authored line, it never shrinks*, and a content-driven size change is the runtime resize §5.1 and §6 both forbid. **What is left is COMPOSITION and SPAN, which is where every other version of this problem landed today.** **Scope, stated so it is not read wider than it is: this governs AMOUNTS** — not counts, ordinals, clocks or scores — and it does not forbid a non-money aggregate elsewhere choosing a compact form on its own ruling | batch 57 |
